Existing systems typically rely on entangled acoustic representations, which allow the underlying language model to depend excessively on recovered lexical content instead of grounding its behavior in acoustic-prosodic evidence. We address this limitation with EMODE, an emotion-aware speech language model built around \textbf{Dynamic Para-Semantic Experts (DPSE)}. DPSE decomposes continuous speech features into semantic and paralinguistic pathways, routes them dynamically, and fuses them before integration into the language model. To turn this structural decomposition into functional specialization, EMODE is trained with a three-stage curriculum consisting of semantic warm-up, paralinguistic activation, and joint refinement, guided by Orthogonal Expert Guidance (OEG), Semantic-to-Acoustic Alignment (SAA), and Gating Diversity Regularization (GDR). Experiments on SER test, empathetic response evaluation, and the newly constructed bilingual MEPA benchmark show that EMODE improves the balance between lexical fidelity and emotional sensitivity, strengthens affect-grounded response generation, and exposes the value of explicit para-semantic factorization for robust cross-corpus emotion understanding.

</details>


### [137] [Verdicts Without Annotated Evidence: Rejection Sampling or Label-Only Post-Training for Evidence Recovery?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06962)
*Nishanth Nayakanti,Prasang Gupta,Ashutosh Bilthare,Kevin Paul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究探索仅用裁决标签后训练小语言模型，能否在无人标注证据的情况下恢复审查证据，并比较标签-only训练与拒绝采样的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多审查流程只保留裁决结果，其背后的证据段落未被标注，因为标注证据的成本远高于记录决定。作者希望衡量小模型仅凭裁决记录能恢复多少证据。

Method: 在ContractNLI上，将人工证据跨度留作评估，不对证据进行任何人工标注。仅用裁决标签对小型语言模型进行后训练，并采用拒绝采样：仅保留裁决与记录一致的生成轨迹，再用自动源接地分数挑选。

Result: 六个系统中，匹配裁决与匹配证据跨度仅弱相关且排序不同。标签-only训练达到准确率0.896、跨度F1 0.564；拒绝采样达到0.797和0.556（训练前为0.747和0.493）。逐字引用率在标签-only训练下从0.597升至0.729，拒绝采样下升至0.701。

Conclusion: 单一语料上的单一种子不足以判断哪种方法更优，但两种方法都能在无人标注证据的情况下改善证据恢复。准确率在需要可复核引用时并非可靠指标。

Abstract: In many review workflows the verdict is the only thing retained. The passages behind it are not marked, because that annotation costs far more than recording the decision. We measure how much of that evidence a small language model can recover when it is post-trained on the verdicts alone, with no human evidence labels at any stage. On ContractNLI the human evidence spans are held out until evaluation. Matching the recorded verdict and agreeing with those spans are not the same thing: across six systems the two scores are only weakly related and rank the systems differently, so accuracy is a poor guide when the citations have to be reviewable. Label-only training on the bare verdict reaches accuracy 0.896 and span F1 0.564. Rejection sampling, which keeps a generated trace only when its verdict matches the record and then picks one by an automatic source-grounding score, reaches 0.797 and 0.556, against 0.747 and 0.493 before training. Verbatim citation rises from 0.597 to 0.729 under label-only training and to 0.701 under rejection sampling. One seed on one corpus cannot say which method is better, but both improve the evidence without anyone annotating it.

</details>


### [138] [WavePrune: One period is often enough for RoPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06963)
*Guancheng Du,Luotian Huang,Shaowen Wang,Si Li,Kaifeng Lyu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WavePrune通过将RoPE每个通道限制在首个旋转周期内，消除位置混叠，提升长上下文性能并获得推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoPE虽使注意力对位置整体平移不变，但其旋转具有周期性，导致相隔一个完整旋转周期的相对位置难以区分（位置混叠），从而在注意力图中引入干扰，损害长上下文表现。

Method: 提出WavePrune，将每个通道限制在其第一个旋转周期内，形成滑动窗口式的细粒度稀疏结构，并设计硬件对齐的CUDA内核以利用该稀疏性。

Result: 在五个测试模型中的四个上无需额外调优即提升HELMET分数（Qwen3-8B上35.7→40.0）；从头预训练时外推长度下验证损失更低；在32K上下文下相比FlashAttention-2实现1.15倍prefill和1.24倍decoding加速。

Conclusion: RoPE的周期结构被广泛视为必需，但结果表明其在首个旋转周期之外基本是冗余的。

Abstract: Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) encodes token positions by rotating each two-dimensional channel of the query and key vectors at a channel-specific frequency, making the attention logits invariant to a common shift of positions. However, this rotation is periodic, and it leads to position aliasing where relative positions separated by a full rotation period become hard to tell apart. To address this, we propose WavePrune, which restricts each channel to its first rotation period. We show that it removes the distractions in attention maps created by position aliasing and improves overall long-context performance. Specifically, WavePrune raises the HELMET score on four of five models we test without any extra tuning (e.g., 35.7 -> 40.0 on Qwen3-8B). When pretraining models from scratch, WavePrune also achieves lower validation loss at extrapolated lengths than pretraining without it. Because WavePrune restricts each channel to a sliding window, it induces a fine-grained sparsity that our hardware-aligned CUDA kernels exploit for 1.15x prefill and 1.24x decoding speedups over FlashAttention-2 at 32K context. Together, these results show that RoPE's periodic structure, widely regarded as essential, is largely redundant beyond the first rotation period.

</details>


### [139] [Calibrated Answers About Randomized Trials From a 4-Billion-Parameter Open Model: A Registered Test and a License-Clean Release](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07019)
*Johann Emmanuel Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Fiorillo v0.5 是一个开源模型，针对随机对照试验证据推断任务，输出三类结局方向的概率，并通过预设四项标准后发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文动机是构建一个可公开复用、校准良好、能自动判定干预对结局是否显著影响的证据推断模型，并以预先注册的评估标准决定是否发布。

Method: 以 Qwen3-4B-Base 为基础，加入低秩适配器和决策头，仅在 2,657 篇训练文章中许可允许复用的 1,431 篇上针对 Evidence Inference 2.0 微调；输入文章截断至 6,144 tokens。

Result: 在 EI 测试集（333 篇、1,218 个提示）上，期望校准误差 0.0168 低于 0.05；对数损失比先验低 0.8603，比 Gemma 4 31B-it 低 0.1829；宏 F1 为 0.9248。无文章时宏 F1 降至 0.4384，仅标题可提升 0.0939，交换干预与对照会反转 0.6652 的方向答案。

Conclusion: 四项预设标准全部通过，发布版本与实际评估预测一致，模型以 Apache License 2.0 开源发布。

Abstract: Fiorillo v0.5 is an open model that answers typed questions with a probability for each answer. Its main specialist reads a randomized trial's article, cut to 6,144 tokens, and answers whether an intervention significantly increased, significantly decreased or did not significantly change an outcome against a comparator (Evidence Inference 2.0, EI). It is Qwen3-4B-Base with low-rank adapters and a decision head, fine-tuned for EI only on the 1,431 of 2,657 training articles whose own license allows reuse. Four criteria registered on the Open Science Framework before this version's test predictions decided its release, the second bar judged on EI's test split, whose labels are public. On that split (1,218 prompts in 333 articles), the expected calibration error was 0.0168 against a limit of 0.05; log loss was below the prior's by 0.8603 (95 percent interval 0.8104 to 0.9078) and below that of Gemma 4 31B-it, reading the same input, by 0.1829 (0.1164 to 0.2598); and macro-F1 was 0.9248 against 0.8668, so all four criteria passed. Training the same recipe on clean articles alone cost 0.0123 in accuracy (0.0034 to 0.0207; descriptive). With no article, macro-F1 fell to 0.4384; the title alone raised it by 0.0939 (0.0655 to 0.1234), which a title stating the result or recall of the trial could explain; exchanging intervention and comparator reversed 0.6652 of its direction answers. Run as released, the files matched the evaluated predictions within limits set in advance. The release is under the Apache License 2.0 (digital object identifier 10.57967/hf/10722).

</details>


### [140] [Investigating Model Compression for Neural Machine Translation in the Biomedical Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07032)
*Maria Zafar,Souhail Bakkali,Rejwanul Haque*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究知识蒸馏与量化联合应用于法英生物医学翻译，在保持翻译质量的同时实现模型压缩、推理加速与碳排放降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练Transformer在机器翻译中表现优异，但其部署成本高。知识蒸馏和量化是常用的压缩手段，但在专业领域和低资源条件下均存在局限：蒸馏受限于领域平行语料稀缺，量化会随比特精度下降而导致性能退化。作者希望探究二者联合在资源受限的生物医学翻译场景中的可行性。

Method: 以法英生物医学翻译为任务，采用知识蒸馏与量化相结合的方式，将大型教师模型的知识传递给更小的学生模型。作者设计并比较了多种微调策略，使压缩后的学生模型适应这一专业术语密集、平行资源有限的领域。核心是协同蒸馏与量化的学生模型构建流程。

Result: 相比原始基线模型，协同蒸馏并量化的学生模型体积减少69%，推理速度提升98.21%，CO2排放降低98.46%，且翻译质量未出现明显损失。

Conclusion: 联合优化的压缩技术在专业领域低资源条件下能够产出高效且高性能的模型，适合资源受限的翻译服务提供方使用，为可持续的机器翻译部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large-scale pretrained transformer models have achieved state-of-the-art performance across diverse machine translation tasks, including multilingual settings. Knowledge distillation has emerged as a sustainable approach for model compression, transferring knowledge from large teacher models to smaller, more efficient student models. Similarly, quantization, which reduces the numerical precision of model weights and activations (e.g., from 32-bit to 8-bit representations) is widely used to accelerate inference, enabling models to run several times faster during deployment. However, both techniques face limitations when applied to specialized domain data, particularly under low-resource conditions. In knowledge distillation, the effectiveness of transfer is often constrained by the scarcity of domain-specific parallel data, while quantization can lead to performance degradation as bit precision decreases. In this work, we investigate the combined application of knowledge distillation and quantization for French-to-English biomedical translation, a domain characterized by specialized terminology and limited parallel resources. We develop and compare multiple fine-tuning strategies to adapt compressed student models to this challenging setting. Our experiments demonstrate that a collaboratively distilled and quantized student model achieves a 69% reduction in size, a 98.21% increase in inference speed, and a 98.46% reduction in CO2 emissions compared to the original baseline all without sacrificing translation quality. These results indicate that jointly optimized compression techniques can yield efficient, high-performance models suitable for translation service providers operating under resource constraints.

</details>


### [141] [Turnslide: Scalable Multi-Turn Data Synthesis by Walking a Finite-State Machine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07070)
*Aaron Fainman,Gabriela Kadlecová,Maciej Gryka,Bartosz Kruszczyński,Usman Zafar,Cédric Archambeau,Aaron Klein,David Salinas,Selim Nowicki,Jacek Golebiowski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种全自动轻量级合成框架，将每个 API 建模为有限状态机（FSM），以低成本生成多轮工具调用训练数据，微调小语言模型（SLM）后显著提升下游准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型部署成本低且可运行于私有基础设施，但基础模型在多轮工具调用上表现不佳，而微调又缺少按 API 划分的数据。现有合成方法需要为不同领域搭建模拟运行环境，且每生成一轮对话都需多次调用大模型，成本过高，难以支撑大规模微调。

Method: 将每个 API 建模为有限状态机，用抽象状态表示系统，确定各工具可被调用的时机，从而生成状态合法的工具序列；再通过单次 LLM 调用将工具序列转化为完整训练样本。不追求多样性优化，而是对轮数、工具序列和任务复杂度设定目标分布。

Result: 在生成的轨迹上微调 SLM 来评估数据质量。相比未经变异的基线，基于 FSM 的生成显著提升下游准确率；与现有工作相比，完全准确率达到 70.7%（对比 63.4% 和 53.7%），且 token 消耗减少 3.6–6.6 倍。

Conclusion: 基于有限状态机的轻量级合成框架能够在无需模拟环境和大量 LLM 调用的前提下，高效生成高质量多轮工具调用数据，显著提升小语言模型的下游表现并降低 token 成本。

Abstract: Small language models are inexpensive to serve and can run on private infrastructure, but base models are often not good enough at multi-turn tool calling, and fine-tuning them needs per-API data that rarely exists. Existing synthesis methods are too expensive for high-scale fine-tuning, as they often require mock operational environments for different domains and multiple LLM calls per generated conversation turn. We introduce a fully automated, lightweight synthesis framework that models each API as a finite-state machine, representing the system as abstract states that determine when each tool may be called, producing state-valid sequences of tools; sequences are translated into complete examples with a single LLM call. Rather than optimize diversity, we set a target distribution over the number of turns, the tool sequence and task complexity. We measure data quality by fine-tuning SLMs on generated trajectories, showing that our FSM-based generation significantly improves downstream accuracy over an unmutated baseline and, against existing works, reaches 70.7% full accuracy over 63.4% and 53.7% with 3.6-6.6$\times$ fewer tokens.

</details>


### [142] [JudgeMoE: Distributional Aggregation for LLM-as-a-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07109)
*Yiqi Liu,Joseph James,Yang Wang,Kun Zhao,Chenghao Xiao,Chenghua Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: JudgeMoE是一种轻量聚合器，通过对缓存的judge分数分布赋予样本特定权重并在最终打分前融合，改善了LLM judge评分的聚合效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量压缩后的LLM judge评分会丢失分数分布中的不确定性与分歧信息，同时评分范围选择在不同judge-数据集设置下不稳定。

Method: 提出JudgeMoE，对缓存的judge分数分布分配样本特定权重，先融合分布再计算最终分数；并开展协议研究，考察分数范围与软/硬打分解码。

Result: 在原始10单元基准上，JudgeMoE较均匀log池化平均Spearman提升+0.079；扩展到16个单元后，较最强局部单judge平均提升+0.0393，12/16单元为正，单侧Wilcoxon符号秩检验p=0.0091。

Conclusion: 软打分通常优于硬解码，但最优聚合方法依赖任务与judge池，JudgeMoE在多数设置下带来稳定提升。

Abstract: When an LLM judge scores an output, its score distribution retains uncertainty and disagreement information that is lost after scalar compression. We introduce JudgeMoE, a lightweight aggregator that assigns example-specific weights to cached judge score distributions and fuses them before computing a final score. A protocol study shows that score-range choice is unstable across judge--dataset settings and that soft scoring usually outperforms hard decoding. On the original 10-cell benchmark, JudgeMoE improves mean Spearman over uniform log pooling by $+0.079$. Applying the same configuration to six additional cells yields a $+0.0393$ mean gain over the strongest local single judge across 16 cells, with positive differences in 12/16 cells and a one-sided Wilcoxon signed-rank $p=0.0091$. Validation-based analyses further show that the preferred aggregation method depends on the task and judge pool.

</details>


### [143] [CroissantMiner: Automated Extraction and Validation of Croissant Metadata for ML Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07132)
*Berke Arda,Ahmetcan Yavuz,Paul Gerry,Sebastian Lobentanzer,Nobin Sarwar,Joan Giner-Miguelez,Kongtao Chen,Luyao Zhang,Mrinmaya Sachan,Mubashara Akhtar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向Croissant数据集元数据schema的端到端抽取基准，包含602篇论文（102篇人工标注金标+500篇LLM银标），评测多种模型与智能体架构，发现单次全上下文抽取优于四种分解式智能体架构，长篇RAI字段抽取仍远未可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Croissant已成为机器可读数据集元数据标准，但填充其字段仍依赖人工、需仔细阅读数据集文档，成本高昂，且此前缺乏与社区标准schema对齐的端到端元数据抽取评测基准。

Method: 构建含602篇论文的基准（102篇人工验证金标、500篇LLM生成银标），覆盖Croissant完整schema及核心字段与Responsible AI（RAI）字段；评测前沿模型、开放权重模型与智能体架构等多类抽取系统，采用结合规则打分与经人工审核筛选的LLM评审的两级评测框架。

Result: 单次抽取持续优于所评测的四种智能体架构：在各backbone上，分解式变体准确率均低于单次全上下文抽取；最大差距出现在长篇RAI字段，这类字段需综合并解读散布于论文各处的信息而非单点复制，现有系统可靠性仍远不足。

Conclusion: 单次全上下文抽取在当前任务上更稳健，分解式智能体架构未见增益，长篇RAI字段是主要瓶颈；作者开源基准、评测代码、评审审核、在线demo及面向新系统的排行榜。

Abstract: Croissant has emerged as a standard for machine-readable dataset metadata, yet populating its fields remains labor-intensive and requires careful reading of accompanying dataset documentation. We present the first benchmark enabling end-to-end evaluation of metadata extraction aligned with a community-standard schema. The benchmark comprises 602 papers, including 102 with human-validated gold annotations and 500 with LLM-generated silver annotations, covering the full Croissant schema with both core and Responsible AI (RAI) fields. Using this benchmark, we evaluate a range of extraction systems spanning frontier models, open-weight models, and agentic architectures, under a two-tier evaluation framework that combines rule-based scoring with an LLM judge selected via human audit. We find that single-pass extraction consistently outperforms the four agentic architectures we evaluate: across backbones, these decomposed variants achieve lower accuracy than a single full-context pass. The largest gap appears on long-form RAI fields, which require synthesizing and interpreting information scattered across a paper rather than copying it from a single location, a setting where current systems remain far from reliable. We release the benchmark, evaluation code, judge audit, a live demo, and a leaderboard open to new systems.

</details>


### [144] [Identifying Introspection From the Inside](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07186)
*David I. Atkinson,Dillon Plunkett,David Bau*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过低秩适配器训练模型为虚构角色做决策，发现隐式决策任务训练中会自发涌现准确自我报告现象，其伴随偏好表征向早期层迁移的结构变化，且可用归因相似度这一机制特征区分忠实的与不忠实的自我报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型对自身的陈述既具后果性，又越来越难以仅凭行为验证。如何区分貌似合理的虚构（confabulation）与真正的内省，是一个重要难题。本文希望在受控环境中找到"忠实自我报告"的机制特征。

Method: 使用低秩适配器（LoRA），训练模型按潜在线性偏好函数为虚构角色做决策。在无显式自我报告监督的隐式决策任务上持续微调，观察准确自我报告的自发涌现。随后采用两组手段分析：权重消融与冻结层实验以考察表征位置变化；归因修补（attribution patching）以比较决策任务与自我报告任务之间的归因相似度。

Result: 持续微调隐式决策任务可使模型在无显式监督下涌现出对所学偏好的准确自我报告。权重消融与冻结层实验表明，偏好表征随训练向更早的层迁移。归因修补显示，忠实模型的决策任务与自我报告任务之间的归因相似度显著更高。

Conclusion: 在此受限设定下，忠实自我报告可能要求偏好表征位于既有言语化机制可访问的位置；归因相似度可作为不需理解报告内容本身即可识别忠实性的机制特征。以往研究仅在行为层面观察到模型可忠实或不忠实，本文提出可通过考察网络自身结构来区分这两种计算模式。

Abstract: Large language models make claims about themselves that are both consequential and increasingly difficult to verify from behavior alone. How can we distinguish plausible confabulations from genuine introspection? In this paper, we identify mechanistic signatures of faithful self-report in a controlled setting. Using low-rank adapters, we train models to make decisions on behalf of fictitious characters, according to latent linear preference functions. We find sustained fine-tuning on an implicit decision task can lead to the emergence of accurate self-reporting of models' learned preferences, even without explicit self-report supervision. We ask two research questions about this emergent phenomenon. First: is the emergence of accurate self-reporting accompanied by a measurable structural change in the model? Weight ablations and frozen-layer experiments together indicate that preference representations shift to earlier layers over training, consistent with the hypothesis that faithful self-report requires preferences to be located where pre-existing verbalization mechanisms can access them. Second: can these structural differences distinguish faithful models from unfaithful ones? Using attribution patching, we find that faithful models exhibit significantly higher attribution similarity between the decision-making and self-report tasks -- a mechanistic signature of faithful self-report that does not require us to understand the content of the report itself. Previous work on self-report has observed behaviorally that models can be faithful or unfaithful; our work proposes that, at least in our restricted setting, it is possible to distinguish between the two patterns of computation by examining the structure of the networks themselves.

</details>


### [145] [TIDE 2.0: an open, model-agnostic engine for keyed de-identification of clinical notes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07224)
*Jose D. Posada,Somalee Datta,Priya Desai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 TIDE 2.0——一个 MIT 许可的开源去标识化引擎，将识别与匿名化分为两个可分离阶段，采用无链接表的加密代理生成、按患者间隔保持的日期偏移以及密钥隔离机制，并发布蒸馏自大语言模型的识别器 TIDE2-Sentry。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床笔记记录了患者诊疗的大部分内容，但其中包含受保护健康信息（PHI），在去标识化前无法用于研究。作者指出，去标识化常被当作单纯的检测问题来处理，而仅做检测并不充分：遮蔽会连带删除临床内容，日期置空会破坏纵向分析所需的时间间隔，每次出现都分配新的随机代理值则会切断同一患者不同笔记之间的关联。因此需要一种既保护隐私又保留研究可用性的方案，且应能在机构自有硬件上运行。

Method: TIDE 2.0 采用两个可分离阶段：可替换的识别器与带密钥的匿名化器，二者均可在机构自有硬件上运行。代理值通过密码学方式生成，不存储链接表；日期按每位患者进行保持时间间隔的偏移；在给定密钥下，同一值在所有出现位置获得相同代理；新密钥生成的发布版本无法与先前版本关联。作者同时发布 TIDE2-Sentry，一个从大语言模型蒸馏得到的识别器。

Result: 在两个机构的两个金标准标注语料上，默认配置达到域内 span 级召回率 0.88，在第二个机构语料上为 0.77，精确率分别为 0.88 和 0.87。作者还报告了各细分类别的召回率与精确率。

Conclusion: 该引擎开源，识别器通过受限研究用途协议提供，使各机构能够在自身环境中运行、审查和扩展二者，从而在去标识化过程中兼顾隐私保护与研究可用性。

Abstract: Clinical notes capture most of what is documented about a patient's care, but they cannot be used for research until protected health information (PHI) is removed. De-identification is often treated as a detection problem. Detection alone is not sufficient: redaction strips clinical content along with identifiers, date blanking destroys the temporal intervals needed for longitudinal analysis, and assigning a fresh random surrogate at each occurrence breaks links between a patient's notes. We present TIDE 2.0, an MIT-licensed engine with two separable stages: an interchangeable recognizer and a keyed anonymizer. Both run on hardware the institution owns. Surrogates are generated cryptographically with no stored linkage table. Dates shift by a per-patient, interval-preserving offset; each value receives the same surrogate across all occurrences under a given key; and a release produced under a new key cannot be linked to earlier releases. We also release TIDE2-Sentry, a recognizer distilled from a large language model. On two gold-annotated corpora from two institutions, the default configuration reached span-level recall of 0.88 in-domain and 0.77 on the second institution's corpus, at precision 0.88 and 0.87. We report recall and precision per category alongside these aggregates. The engine is open source, and the recognizer is available under a gated research-use agreement, so institutions can run, inspect and extend both within their own environments.

</details>


### [146] [Kurate: Scalable Scientific Quality Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07306)
*Matthew J. Vowels,Jamie Cummins*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Kurate系统，利用大语言模型（LLM）结合论文及相关文档（如试验注册、研究方案）自动评估已发表研究的质量，并将判断链接到支撑该判断的文本片段。系统应用于4,347篇论文（其中3,913篇报告随机试验），从8个研究设计与报告维度打分。结果显示统计功效、选择性报告和分析预设问题最普遍；与60份专家标注文档对比，提取信息一致性AC1分别为0.94和0.81，证明大规模质量评估可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学检索系统能找出与问题相关的论文，但一般不评估这些论文所提供证据的质量。作者希望实现对已发表研究大规模、可溯源的质量评估，以支持元科学研究问题。

Method: Kurate使用大语言模型，同时输入论文及其相关文档（试验注册、研究方案等），对每篇论文在8个维度上评分：统计功效、因果识别、预注册、选择性报告、测量效度、分析预设、报告透明度、利益冲突与资金来源；并将每个判断链接到其依据的文本段落。系统应用于4,347篇论文语料。

Result: 语料中论文最常在统计功效、选择性报告和分析预设方面存在问题，但不同临床领域的平均质量有差异。与60份留存临床试验文档的专家标注相比，Kurate提取信息在方案评分点匹配221/242，结果发表匹配294/370，AC1分别为0.94和0.81。以一个高质量临床试验为案例，展示了单篇论文总体评级如何分解为各具体判断并链接到注册、方案和发表报告中的具体证据。

Conclusion: 大规模研究质量评估是可行的，并可用于回答元科学研究问题。

Abstract: Scientific search systems can find papers that are relevant to a question, but they generally do not assess the quality of the evidence that those papers provide. We present Kurate, a system that uses large language models (LLMs) to assess the quality of published studies. Kurate uses both the paper and its related documents (e.g., the study's trial registration and protocol), and links each of its judgments to the passage of text on which that judgment is based. We applied Kurate to a corpus of 4,347 papers (3,913 of which report randomized trials) and scored each paper on 8 dimensions of study design and reporting: specifically, statistical power, causal identification, preregistration, selective reporting, measurement validity, analysis prespecification, reporting transparency, and conflict of interest and funding. Across the corpus, we found that papers most often exhibited issues with statistical power, selective reporting, and analysis prespecification, although average quality differed between clinical areas. When compared against expert annotations of 60 held-out clinical-trial documents, the information Kurate extracted matched the expert label in 221/242 protocol scorepoints and 294/370 results-publication scorepoints, with AC1 0.94 and 0.81, respectively. Using a well-reputed, high quality clinical trial as a worked example, we show how a single paper's overall grade breaks down into separate judgments, with each linked to specific evidence from the trial's registration, protocol, and published report. Together, these results show that large-scale quality assessment of this kind is feasible, and that it can be used to address meta-scientific research questions.

</details>


### [147] [Who Wrote It Is Not Enough: Detecting Who Contributed the Insight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07365)
*Zhuoyang Zou,Abolfazl Ansari,Jiaxi Yang,Delvin Ce Zhang,Qian Chen,Dongwon Lee,Wenpeng Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出"洞见溯源"任务，判断评审洞见的来源是人类、LLM还是混合贡献，并构建了InsightProv-v0数据集与两阶段对抗框架以抑制捷径信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益参与科学写作与同行评审，仅判断文本由谁书写已不够，更需要识别背后的"洞见"究竟源自人类、LLM还是二者混合。现有方法在原始数据上的高性能可能具有误导性，因为模型会依赖语言与文本作者身份等捷径特征。

Method: 基于4,057篇科学论文与12,660条人类评审构建InsightProv-v0，用GPT-4o、Gemini、DeepSeek模拟不同LLM参与程度，并在句子级别标注来源。随后提出两阶段对抗框架，在抑制捷径信号的同时保留与来源相关的信息。

Result: 模型在原始数据上表现强劲，但在逐步去偏的评估下性能大幅下降，说明其依赖捷径特征；两阶段对抗框架可显著缓解该问题。分析显示论文依据与相邻评审上下文提供互补的来源信号，且人类、混合、AI洞见在信息来源与失败模式上存在系统性差异。

Conclusion: AI洞见多停留在泛化或论文已有信息上，而人类洞见更常引入外部知识与独立判断。这表明措辞虽可被LLM改写，但"思想来源"会留下更深层、更持久的信号。

Abstract: As LLMs increasingly assist scientific writing and peer review, detecting who wrote the text is no longer sufficient: we need to determine who contributed the underlying insight. We introduce Insight Provenance, the task of identifying whether a review insight originates from a human, an LLM, or their hybrid contribution. We construct InsightProv-v0 from 4,057 scientific papers and 12,660 human reviews, simulating different levels of LLM involvement with GPT-4o, Gemini, and DeepSeek and annotating provenance at the sentence level. We show that strong performance on raw data can be misleading, as models exploit linguistic and textual-authorship shortcuts that degrade substantially under progressively debiased evaluation. We therefore propose a two-stage adversarial framework that suppresses shortcut signals while preserving provenance-relevant information. Beyond detection, extensive analyses reveal what makes intellectual authorship identifiable: paper grounding and neighboring review context provide complementary provenance signals, while human, hybrid, and AI insights systematically differ in their information sources and failure modes. Most strikingly, AI insights predominantly remain close to generic or paper-provided information, whereas human insights more often introduce external knowledge and independent judgment. These findings suggest that while wording can be rewritten by an LLM, the provenance of an idea leaves a deeper and more persistent signal.

</details>


### [148] [AccentCL: Robust Accent Classification with Incremental Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07426)
*Mu-Ruei Tseng,Waris Quamer,Ghady Nasrallah,Ricardo Gutierrez-Osuna*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AccentCL 提出一种面向英语口音分类的类增量学习框架，结合冻结 Whisper 编码器、不平衡感知损失、域均值对齐与回放式持续学习，在五类任务及增量新增口音类别上取得稳健性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统口音分类器使用固定标签集合，无法适应新出现的口音类别；同时口音语音语料常存在严重类别不平衡，以及由录音条件差异导致的跨语料域偏移问题，因此需要一个能同时应对类别不平衡、域偏移并支持类别增量扩展的框架。

Method: AccentCL 从冻结的 Whisper-Large-v3 编码器提取多层表示，采用不平衡感知交叉熵损失以减轻对多数类口音的偏置，并引入域均值对齐损失来减小不同训练语料间的分布均值偏移；标签空间则通过基于回放的持续学习扩展，利用冻结基础模型保持旧知识，并使用旧到新间隔损失来减少对新类别的过度预测。

Result: 在五类口音分类任务上，AccentCL 达到 77.1% 平衡准确率和 76.9% 宏平均 F1。增量加入西班牙口音英语时，新类 F1 为 83.3%，基类平衡准确率保持 77.3%；随后加入中国口音英语时，新类 F1 为 61.8%，已学类别平衡准确率保持 77.6%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，AccentCL 能实现稳健的地区口音分类，并支持在不进行完整重训练的情况下增量添加新的口音类别。

Abstract: Accent classifiers are typically trained with a fixed label inventory and cannot accommodate new accent categories as new data becomes available. Moreover, accented speech corpora often exhibit substantial class imbalance and/or domain shift due to differences in recording conditions across corpora. We present AccentCL, a class-incremental learning framework for English accent classification that is robust to class imbalance and cross-corpus domain shift. AccentCL extracts multi-layer representations from a frozen Whisper-Large-v3 encoder, optimized with an imbalance-aware cross-entropy loss to reduce bias toward the majority accent classes and a domain mean alignment loss that minimizes distributional mean shift across training corpora. The label space is then expanded via replay-based continual learning, using the frozen base model for knowledge retention and an old-to-new margin loss to reduce overprediction on newly added classes. On a five-class accent classification task, AccentCL achieves 77.1% balanced accuracy and a 76.9% macro-averaged F1 score. We further evaluate the model's ability to incrementally incorporate two new accent categories: Spanish-accented and Chinese-accented English. When adding Spanish-accented English to the pretrained model, AccentCL attains an F1 of 83.3% on the new class while retaining 77.3% balanced accuracy on the base classes. When subsequently adding Chinese-accented English, it achieves 61.8% F1 on the new class while preserving 77.6% balanced accuracy on the previously learned classes. These results show that AccentCL enables robust regional accent classification while allowing new accent categories to be added without full retraining.

</details>


### [149] [Closing Ambient Clinical Documentation Gaps with Automated Provider Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07502)
*Joseph Paul Cohen,Raj Shah,Han-Chin Shing,Fang Wang,Susan Nguyen,Chaitanya Shivade,Jack Moriarty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM能否自动化临床文档中的查询闭环（DAU：起草-提问-更新），覆盖病历起草、ICD-10编码和医嘱提取三项任务。作者基于3,000次真实就诊审计构建了五个转录退化基准，并分析21k轮真实澄清对话，发现有效提问的预测因子具有任务特异性，约9%的对话轮次反而损害表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床文档专家需向医生发送"provider queries"以填补临床记录缺口，确保计费准确。既有工作虽已自动化病历起草、ICD-10编码与医嘱提取，但都假设转录稿完整，忽视了这些缺口，因此需要研究能否用LLM实现查询闭环的自动化。

Method: 审计3,000次真实就诊以识别文档缺失来源，并在公开数据上构建五个转录退化基准；分析21k轮真实对话澄清轮次，探究有用提问的预测因子及其任务差异性。

Result: 有用提问的预测因子具有任务特异性：oracle置信度占主导，但病历完整性只需简单的回忆式问题，而ICD-10编码需要更难的多选项问题。约9%的轮次反而降低性能，源于冗余提问和仍触发重写的非回答。

Conclusion: 查询闭环的部署不仅取决于"该问什么"，同样取决于"何时不该问"。

Abstract: Provider queries are clarifying requests sent by clinical documentation specialists to physicians to close gaps in the clinical note and ensure accurate billing. Prior work automates note drafting, ICD-10 coding, and order extraction assuming a complete transcript, leaving these gaps unaddressed. We study whether an LLM can automate the query loop, termed DAU (Draft, Ask, Update), across those three tasks. An audit of 3,000 real visits identifies the sources of missing documentation, from which we build five transcript-degradation benchmarks on public data. Analyzing 21k clarification turns on real conversations, we find useful-question predictors are task-specific: oracle confidence dominates, but note completeness needs only simple recall questions while ICD-10 coding needs harder, multi-option ones. About 9% of turns hurt performance, driven by redundant questions and non-answers that still trigger a rewrite. Deployment depends on learning "when not" as much as "what to" ask.

</details>


### [150] [Not What a Child Expressed: Auditing the Sign-to-Text Safety Interface in Child-Facing AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07519)
*Muhammad Rafiullah Memon,Viet Vo,Wanlun Ma,Yang Xiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出对"手语翻译(SLT)系统与未成年人安全防护机制交界处"的聋人视角部署前审计方案，包含失败分类法、情景模式、四种比较条件与四项结果指标，以澳大利亚手语(Auslan)为首个案例研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLT已进入消费级产品，用于将美国手语转为英文文本；而面向儿童的人工智能与平台信任安全工具（未成年人账户过滤器、诱拐/骚扰分类器）需要依据文本做出判断。使用SLT的聋童由此"经翻译"抵达这些安全防护。然而作者未发现任何公开记录显示两者被联合评估过，且主流已部署的SLT模型既未在18岁以下手语者上训练，也未在其上做过正式评估。翻译中若出现否定、参与者角色、保密性、紧迫性或求助意图的偏差，可能在不影响流畅度的前提下改变安全判定。

Method: 提出一套由聋人群体参与设计的部署前审计方案，针对SLT与儿童安全防护的交界处，包含：失败分类法(failure taxonomy)、经脱敏处理的情景模式(scenario schema)、四种比较条件、四项结果度量指标。计划以澳大利亚手语(Auslan)作为首个案例研究。

Result: 本文为方案性/立场性论文，未报告实证结果；核心发现是文献与已部署系统中缺乏对SLT与儿童安全防护的联合评估，以及该类模型在未成年手语者上的训练与评估空白。

Conclusion: SLT与儿童安全机制的组合存在未被评估的隐性风险，尤其语义关键要素（否定、角色、保密、紧迫性、求助）的翻译偏差可能改变安全决策。应以聋人主导、部署前的审计框架来填补这一空白，并以Auslan为首个实证案例。

Abstract: Automatic sign language translation (SLT) has entered consumer products, turning American Sign Language into English text for dictation, messaging, and queries put to a conversational assistant. Child-facing AI and platform trust-and-safety tooling decide on text, using filters on minor accounts and grooming classifiers that score chat messages. A signing child who uses SLT therefore reaches these safeguards through a translation. We found no publicly documented system in which the two have been jointly evaluated, and the leading deployed SLT model was neither trained nor formally evaluated on signers under 18. Errors that alter negation, participant roles, secrecy, urgency or help-seeking could change a safety decision without disturbing fluency. This paper proposes a Deaf-informed pre-deployment audit of that boundary, with a failure taxonomy, a sanitised scenario schema, four comparison conditions, and four outcome measures. Auslan is the planned first case study.

</details>


### [151] [Quality-Aware Self-Correcting Speech Translation on an Edge Device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07545)
*Zubair Ajmal Farooq,Diptesh Kanojia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出一个可在Jetson Nano(4GB)上完全离线运行的语音到语音翻译流水线，利用多语言BERT相似度作为质量估计(QE)门控，在置信度低于阈值τ时触发二次修正；在1,012句FLORES-200英西数据上，MBR解码(M2, τ=0.90)相对贪心解码在BLEU、ChrF、COMET上均取得统计显著提升，而QE更适合做门控而非重排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的边缘设备（如Jetson Nano）上实现离线语音翻译时，翻译质量往往不够理想，而重新训练模型代价高昂。作者希望在不重训练的前提下，通过质量估计门控识别低质量翻译并自动修正，同时探究QE在候选筛选环节中真正的价值所在。

Method: 系统由Whisper-tiny ASR接Opus-MT翻译器构成；以多语言BERT余弦相似度作为QE门控，当置信度低于阈值τ时启动二次修正。比较三种修正方法：QE重排序(M1)、最小贝叶斯风险解码(M2)、约束束搜索(M3)，并在1,012句FLORES-200英西句子上评测。

Result: M2在τ=0.90时相对贪心解码取得显著提升：BLEU +0.67 (p<0.001)、ChrF +0.51 (p<0.001)、COMET +0.0020 (N=3, p=0.002)。M1无显著增益；M3显著劣于基线(p>0.99)。从候选筛选中移除QE模型(M1→M2)不损害质量，且从关键路径释放680 MB内存。基于后编辑努力文献的增益-编辑比显示，较小候选池(N=3)修正更精细、语义充分性更好，较大池(N=10)则最大化词汇奖励。

Conclusion: 核心发现是QE可有效作为门控，但作为重排序器表现不佳。系统实现完全离线边缘部署，并演示了六种语言对的实时翻译。

Abstract: We present a fully offline speech-to-speech translation pipeline that runs on a Jetson Nano (4 GB) and corrects its own weak translations without retraining. A Whisper-tiny ASR feeds an Opus-MT translator; multilingual BERT cosine similarity acts as a Quality Estimation (QE) gate, triggering a secondary-pass correction when confidence falls below a pre-defined threshold $τ$. We compare three correction methods: QE reranking (M1), Minimum Bayes-Risk decoding (M2), and constrained beam search (M3). On 1,012 FLORES-200 sentences (English-Spanish), M2 at $τ=0.90$ produces statistically significant improvements over greedy decoding on BLEU (+0.67, p<0.001), ChrF (+0.51, p<0.001), and COMET (+0.0020 at N=3, p=0.002); M1 yields no significant gains, and M3 is significantly worse than baseline (p>0.99). Our central finding is that QE functions effectively as a gate but poorly as a ranker: removing the QE model from candidate selection (M1$\to$M2) does not hurt quality and frees 680 MB from the critical path. Using a gain-to-edit ratio adapted from the post-editing-effort literature, we further show that smaller candidate pools (N=3) yield more surgical corrections with better semantic adequacy, while larger pools (N=10) maximise lexical reward. We release the system and demonstrate live translation across six language pairs.

</details>


### [152] [HouseholdBench: Evaluating Large Language Models as Predictors of Household Economic Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07563)
*Jin Huang,Diego Ferreras Garrucho,Yutong Xie,Walter M. Yuan,Qiaozhu Mei,Chen Lian,Jonathon Hazell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 HouseholdBench，整合 6 项美国家庭调查与 32 个预测任务，评估 13 个大语言模型（LLMs）对家庭决策与政策响应的预测能力，并给出提升开源模型性能的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经济学需要能跨多种情境定量建模家庭决策的模型，LLMs 被认为具备这一潜力，但已有评估覆盖的调查与结果较少，且未研究家庭如何随经济条件变化而调整行为。

Method: 构建 HouseholdBench 评估基准，联合 6 项美国家庭调查和 32 个预测任务，涵盖数值、类别与概率结果，涉及消费、收入、劳动、预期和住房。任务基于历史行为、人口特征与宏观经济条件，测试 LLMs 能否预测行为及家庭对政策变化的调整。评估 13 个专有与开源权重 LLMs，对照无变化基线与梯度提升树模型。

Result: 多数 LLMs 优于无变化基线，包括政策响应任务，最佳模型将数值结果误差降低 12.2%。多数任务中梯度提升树排名第一，领先专有 LLMs 接近其表现，开源权重模型落后。LLMs 在不同任务上存在系统性高估与低估。微调并聚合每个观测的 16 个预测，可使 40 亿参数开源模型匹配专有模型表现，且改进可泛化到未参与微调的政策响应任务。

Conclusion: HouseholdBench 提供了更全面的家庭决策预测评估，表明 LLMs 具备一定预测与政策响应能力，但传统树模型仍占优；通过微调与预测聚合可缩小开源与专有模型差距。数据集、代码与排行榜已公开。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have the potential to meet a key goal in economics: a quantitative model of household decision making, across a variety of settings. Yet existing evaluations cover few surveys and outcomes, and do not study how households adjust to changing economic conditions. We introduce a new evaluation, HouseholdBench, which unites 6 U.S. household surveys and 32 prediction tasks spanning numeric, categorical and probabilistic outcomes, related to consumption, income, labor, expectations, and housing. Using past behavior, demographics and macroeconomic conditions, the tasks test whether LLMs predict behavior, including how households adjust to changes in various policies. We evaluate 13 proprietary and open-weight LLMs against a no-change baseline and a gradient-boosted tree model. Most LLMs outperform the no-change baseline, including for policy response tasks -- with the best model lowering error for numeric outcomes by 12.2%. Across most tasks, gradient-boosted trees rank first; leading proprietary LLMs approach their performance, but open-weight models lag. LLMs exhibit systematic over- and underprediction across different tasks. We identify methods that enable a 4 billion parameter open-weight model to match proprietary models' performance: fine-tuning and aggregating 16 predictions per observation. Improvements generalize to policy-response tasks, which are excluded from fine-tuning. We release our datasets, code, and leaderboard on our website: https://jn-huang.github.io/householdbench

</details>


### [153] [Two Vectors Replace In-Context Demos: Structured Task Adaptation via Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07572)
*Xi Ding,Naichen Shi,Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: STAVE用两个任务特定向量替代演示样例，插入输入嵌入，在少样本多模态与文本任务上匹配或超越SOTA，且推理零样本成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文学习（ICL）虽能让冻结的大型多模态模型（LMM）从少量演示中适应新任务，但每个查询都要重新编码演示，每个演示图像带来数百个视觉token，开销大。无演示方法虽用紧凑任务状态降低成本，却存在任务参数随深度增长、插入token或key无法改变层内注意力分配等问题。

Method: 提出STAVE（Structured Task Adaptation via Embeddings），用两个任务特定向量替代演示，并加到现有输入嵌入中：readout向量更新产生答案的token，context向量更新其他结构token组。两者在有演示和无演示的提示上用答案标签训练。

Result: 在六个LMM和五个大语言模型上实验表明，STAVE在多模态任务上以远少任务参数匹配或超越SOTA；在18个文本任务上超越15-shot ICL和现有task vectors，且推理为零样本成本。

Conclusion: STAVE以极小任务参数实现高效任务适应，兼具性能与零样本推理效率，并通过损失一阶分析与margin界提供理论支持。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) adapts frozen large multimodal models (LMMs) to new tasks from a few demonstrations (demos), but re-encodes them at every query, where each demo image adds up to hundreds of visual tokens. Demo-free methods remove this cost with a compact task state. However, they add it at locations searched per task or at every decoder layer, where task parameters grow with depth. Moreover, inserted tokens or keys cannot change how the original prompt divides its attention within a layer. To address these issues, we propose Structured Task Adaptation via Embeddings (STAVE), which replaces demos with two task-specific vectors added to existing input embeddings. Specifically, a readout vector updates the answer-producing tokens and a context vector updates the other structural token groups. Both are trained with answer labels on prompts with and without demos. We justify these design choices theoretically using a first-order analysis of the loss and a margin bound. Extensive experiments on six LMMs and five large language models show that STAVE matches or outperforms state-of-the-art methods on multimodal tasks with far fewer task parameters and surpasses 15-shot ICL and prior task vectors on 18 text tasks, all at zero-shot inference cost.

</details>


### [154] [Large Language Model Orchestration under Heterogeneous Preferences via Explicit Persona Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07587)
*Shuqing Shi,Ziyan Wang,Milind Tambe,Yali Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出HARP框架，将LLM编排器对智能体隐藏偏好的信念从提示词中移出，改为每个智能体维护数值后验并用贝叶斯规则闭式更新，理论上达到与显式联合推断相同的贝叶斯遗憾，HARP+通过加入区分性动作奖励增强推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编排需要协调异构自主智能体，而每个智能体持有不公开的私有偏好。核心挑战在于维护并更新对各智能体偏好的信念。现有LLM编排器将信念以提示文本形式承载，缺乏显式更新规则，导致早期错误持续并传播而无法纠正。

Method: 提出HARP框架，把信念从提示词中移出：为每个智能体在一组有限候选偏好上维护一个数值后验，并用贝叶斯规则以闭式形式更新。语言模型只负责提供动作和各候选的似然，从而将估计与模型推理解耦。进一步提出HARP+，在规划中加入对能区分候选的动作的奖励，使最优动作无信息时推断仍能继续。

Result: 理论证明：在分解精确时，HARP达到与显式联合推断相同的\tilde O(\sqrt K)贝叶斯遗憾。在三种实验基底上（从偏好完全决定收益、到收益还依赖其他因素、再到显式联合推断不可行的规模）验证，HARP+在该理论所刻画的类别中是最强的非oracle方法。

Conclusion: 将偏好信念从提示文本转为显式数值后验可避免错误累积并保持可纠正性，HARP/HARP+在理论与实验中均优于现有LLM编排器，为异构偏好智能体编排提供了可扩展且推断持续有效的方案。

Abstract: LLM orchestration investigates how an orchestrator coordinates a group of autonomous agents to achieve common goals or maximize collective welfare. The agents are typically heterogeneous, each holding a private preference that it pursues but does not reveal. Inferring such hidden preferences from behavior has been a subject of long-standing research in game theory and multi-agent systems. The core challenge lies in maintaining a belief over every agent's preference and updating it from the agents' observed actions. Existing LLM orchestrators carry that belief as prompt text with no explicit update rule. This lets early errors persist and propagate rather than be corrected. We therefore propose \textbf{HARP} (Heterogeneous-preference Agent oRchestration via Preference inference), a novel framework that moves the belief out of the prompt. Specifically, HARP maintains one numeric posterior per agent over a finite set of candidate preferences and updates it in closed form by Bayes' rule. The language model supplies only actions and per-candidate likelihoods, so estimation is decoupled from its reasoning. We prove that HARP attains the same $\tilde O(\sqrt K)$ Bayesian regret as explicit joint inference when the factorization is exact. Furthermore, HARP\textsuperscript{+} augments planning with a bonus for actions that distinguish the candidates, so inference continues even when the optimal action is uninformative. Empirical results on three substrates, ranging from payoffs the preferences fully determine, through payoffs that depend on more than them, to scales where explicit joint inference is infeasible, demonstrate that HARP\textsuperscript{+} is the strongest non-oracle method across the class our theory identifies.

</details>


### [155] [Recurrent Looped Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07591)
*Yifan Zhang,Jichen Feng,Shihan Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出循环环状Transformer（RLT），将层分为并行因果编码器与循环解码器，使计算路径随序列长度增长而单token成本固定，在多算法任务上显著优于标准Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer对每个token施加的计算深度固定，与序列长度无关，难以胜任需要随输入逐步更新的状态跟踪任务。

Method: RLT将八层拆分为并行因果编码器与循环解码器；解码器在每个token处将编码器输出与上一token的最终解码状态合并，形成随长度增长的循环计算路径。在六个算法任务上，比较五种层划分方案与八层Transformer（三个随机种子）。

Result: 仅用至多40位训练，两种RLT划分可将奇偶校验泛化到256位且每个种子均达100%准确率，而Transformer仅为随机水平；在长度八倍的基于交换的S₅置换跟踪上，RLT最终状态准确率达97%（Transformer低于1%），且准确率随解码器深度提升；模运算超出训练长度时RLT最高达93%（Transformer为33%）。消融显示收益依赖反馈：移除反馈使奇偶校验与S₅置换在各划分下均降至随机水平。将反馈改为每四token块更新一次可保留64位奇偶校验99%的准确率，但置换跟踪依赖逐token反馈，分块使长度64的S₅从100%降至20%。

Conclusion: 循环反馈使Transformer获得随序列长度增长的有效深度，是状态跟踪与长度泛化的关键；而不同任务对反馈粒度需求不同。

Abstract: State tracking requires an update at every input, but the depth a Transformer applies to each token is fixed regardless of sequence length. We introduce the Recurrent Looped Transformer (RLT), which splits its layers between a parallel causal encoder and a recurrent decoder. At each token, the decoder merges the encoder output with the previous token's final decoder state, so the computation path grows with sequence length at a fixed per-token cost. On six algorithmic tasks, we compare five splits of eight layers with an eight-layer Transformer over three seeds. Trained on at most 40 bits, two RLT splits generalize parity to 256 bits with 100% accuracy in every seed, while the Transformer stays at chance. On swap-based $S_5$ permutation tracking at eight times the training length, RLT reaches 97% final-state accuracy versus under 1% for the Transformer, and accuracy increases with decoder depth. On modular arithmetic beyond the training lengths, RLT reaches up to 93% versus 33% for the Transformer. Ablations show that these gains depend on the feedback: removing it drops parity and swap-based $S_5$ to chance at every split. Updating the feedback once per four-token chunk lets known tokens in a chunk run in parallel and keeps 64-bit parity at 99%, while permutation tracking depends on per-token feedback: chunking lowers length-64 swap-based $S_5$ from 100% to 20%.

</details>


### [156] [Monte Carlo Estimation for KV Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07643)
*Ahsan Bilal,Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Muhammad Umer,Wajih Hassan Raza,Atta Ul Asad,Young D. Kwon,Michal Valko,Dean F. Hougen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LORE-KV是一种免训练的KV缓存淘汰方法，通过从冻结模型采样短续写并用其响应侧查询状态估计prompt token效用，在LongBench和RULER上取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存淘汰方法关注"读prompt时哪些内存重要"，而忽略了"回答时哪些内存重要"。由于淘汰时解码查询未知，已有未来感知方法依赖伪响应或合成未来查询估计，存在局限。

Method: 将固定预算的未来感知淘汰建模为对模型条件查询轨迹的分布估计，提出LORE-KV：从冻结目标模型采样短自回归续写，用其响应侧查询状态估计prompt token效用；通过投影的留一注意力输出删除代价打分，跨采样未来聚合并可选轨迹加权；临时续写在最终解码前丢弃，无需辅助模型或训练。

Result: B=128时单次响应侧续写恢复约89%增益；LongBench上Qwen2.5-14B平均从45.49升至48.24(+2.75)，16K RULER上Mistral-7B从45.20升至51.05(+5.85)；更大预算下增益减弱且存在任务级回退；相比AnDPro，单样本墙钟时间为1.46-2.77倍。

Conclusion: LORE-KV以一次性压缩开销的代价有效提升缓存淘汰性能，机制清晰，但增益随预算增大而递减并伴随部分任务回退，未来可在效率与稳定性上改进。

Abstract: Most KV-cache eviction methods ask, in effect, which memory appeared important while reading the prompt? We instead ask, which memory will matter while answering? Since decoding queries are unavailable at eviction time, prior future-aware methods rely on pseudo-responses or synthetic future-query estimates. We cast fixed-budget future-aware eviction as distributional estimation over plausible model-conditional query trajectories and introduce LORE-KV (Lookahead Output-perturbation with Reliability-weighted Ensembles for Key-Value caches), a training-free method that samples short autoregressive continuations from the frozen target model and uses their response-side query states to estimate prompt-token utility. Tokens are scored by projected leave-one-out attention-output deletion cost and aggregated across sampled futures with optional trajectory weighting. The temporary continuations are discarded before final decoding, requiring no auxiliary model or training. Ablations isolate the mechanism: at B=128, a single response-side continuation recovers about 89% of the gain over the prompt-window control, while additional futures provide smaller improvements. At B=128, LORE-KV raises the LongBench average on Qwen2.5-14B from 45.49 to 48.24 (+2.75) and the 16K RULER average on Mistral-7B from 45.20 to 51.05 (+5.85). Gains diminish at larger cache budgets and coexist with task-level regressions. LORE-KV incurs 1.46-2.77x AnDPro's per-sample wall-clock time as a one-time compression overhead across six dense and hybrid-attention backbones.

</details>


### [157] [DLoop: Looped Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07659)
*Geonmo Gu,Byeongho Heo,HeeJae Jun,Yoohoon Kang,Sangmin Lee,Sangdoo Yun,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DLoop 提出一种循环式投机解码方法，在验证前自适应执行多个起草阶段，减少目标模型前向次数，墙钟加速提升 5%–41%，且保持无损解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着起草模型能力增强，目标模型经常在一次起草阶段中接受全部 token，但每个起草阶段后仍会执行一次验证，导致不必要的目标模型前向计算，即便起草本可继续。现有自适应起草长度方法主要只对自回归起草模型带来加速；对于并行起草模型，继续起草需要未验证 token 的目标模型隐状态，因而难以直接扩展。

Method: 提出 DLoop，一种循环式投机解码形式：在验证前自适应地执行多个起草阶段。当起草模型保持置信时继续起草，并将累积的全部起草 token 一起验证。采用 loop-aware training，使起草模型在额外起草阶段中接触自身对未验证 token 的隐状态，从而保持可靠性。通过增加起草模型前向计算，减少目标模型为验证所需的前向次数。

Result: 在 EAGLE-3、DFlash、Domino、DSpark 及多 token 预测模块等多种投机解码方法上，DLoop 将墙钟加速提升 5% 至 41%，同时保持无损解码。

Conclusion: DLoop 通过自适应多阶段起草与 loop-aware training，有效降低目标模型验证开销，为并行起草模型等场景提供通用加速，并保持解码结果无损。代码将发布于 https://github.com/naver-ai/DLoop。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation in large language models. In each drafting stage, a lightweight draft model proposes tokens that the target model subsequently verifies. With increasingly capable draft models, we find that the target model frequently accepts all tokens produced in a drafting stage. A verification nevertheless follows each drafting stage, resulting in unnecessary target-model forward passes even when drafting could have continued. Adaptive draft length methods decide during decoding how many draft tokens precede a verification, but they raise the speedup only for autoregressive draft models. For a parallel draft model, drafting further requires target-model hidden states for draft tokens that have not been verified. We propose DLoop, a looped form of speculative decoding that adaptively performs multiple drafting stages before verification. DLoop continues drafting while the draft model remains confident and verifies all accumulated draft tokens together. Loop-aware training keeps the draft model reliable in the additional drafting stages by exposing it to its own hidden states for unverified draft tokens. By spending additional draft-model forward passes, DLoop reduces the number of target-model forward passes required for verification. Across diverse speculative decoding methods including EAGLE-3, DFlash, Domino, DSpark, and multi-token prediction modules, DLoop improves the wall-clock speedup by 5 to 41 percent while preserving lossless decoding. Code will be available at https://github.com/naver-ai/DLoop.

</details>


### [158] [Detecting LLM-Assisted Vietnamese Writing via Keystrokes under Behavioral Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07700)
*Thanh Dong,An Ngo,Minh Dau,Rajesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究击键动力学在检测大语言模型辅助写作中的鲁棒性，构建越南语击键数据集并提出行为威胁模型，发现序列模型优于特征方法，转录可靠识别但改写与对抗样本易误判，采用对抗训练可显著提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型辅助写作的普及，需要可靠方法区分真实写作与LLM辅助写作。击键动力学被认为能反映写作过程，但其在真实写作模式及用户故意改变打字模式等对抗条件下的鲁棒性尚不明确。

Method: 构建越南语击键数据集，涵盖真实写作、转录与改写等写作模式；定义基于行为的威胁模型并生成行为操纵变体以规避检测；评估四类击键建模方法：时间与节奏表示，以及用1D-CNN和TypeNet建模的序列表示，在用户无关与上下文无关设置下测试，并引入基于行为操纵数据的对抗训练。

Result: 序列模型在多数情况下优于基于特征的方法，击键信号包含区分写作过程的判别信息；但检测并非一致鲁棒：转录可被可靠识别，而改写与对抗操纵样本在未显式建模时，常被误判为真实写作。引入对抗训练后，可分性与鲁棒性显著提升。

Conclusion: 基于击键的检测关键取决于是否接触多样化写作行为；在有限条件下取得的强性能若缺乏针对性建模，无法泛化到真实或对抗场景。

Abstract: We study the robustness of keystroke dynamics for detecting large language model (LLM)-assisted writing. We introduce a Vietnamese keystroke dataset capturing realistic writing modes, including bona fide composition, transcription, and paraphrasing. We also define a behaviorally grounded threat model in which users deliberately alter typing patterns. To implement the threat model, we create behaviorally manipulated variants of the data designed to evade keystroke-based detection. We evaluate four keystroke modeling approaches: temporal and rhythmic representations, and sequential representations modeled with a one-dimensional convolutional neural network (1D-CNN) and TypeNet, under user-independent and context-independent settings. The results show that sequential models outperform feature-based approaches in most cases and that keystroke signals encode discriminative information about the writing process. However, detection is not uniformly robust: transcription is reliably identified, while paraphrasing and adversarially manipulated samples are frequently misclassified as bona fide when not explicitly modeled. To address this, we incorporate adversarial training using behaviorally manipulated data, which substantially improves separability and robustness. These results suggest that keystroke-based detection depends critically on exposure to diverse writing behaviors, and that strong performance under limited conditions does not generalize to realistic or adversarial settings without targeted modeling.

</details>


### [159] [Readout Stability in Prefill-Only Decision Models:Zero-Label Prediction and Inference-Time Compute Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07716)
*Ran Li,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文发现并验证了"仅预填充"决策模型的一种可测试性质：当干预仅改变候选菜单而输入文本固定时，干预后准确率可由缓存的首轮分布直接预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式语言模型逐token解码成本高，而受Jev模型启发的仅预填充决策模型在一次前向传播中即可为菜单中所有候选打分，成本低一到两个数量级。作者希望探究这种只读结构是否带来可检验的、可利用的性质，从而把推理期计算转化为部署前即可做出的决策。

Method: 提出基于缓存首轮分布的估计器：将第一遍概率限制在菜单内、重新归一化后取argmax，无需标签、无需第二次前向传播；并在七个模型家族、十个数据集、两类任务上评估菜单级干预的预测误差，与概率级变体及同规模生成式模型对比。

Result: 菜单级干预预测误差在4.2个点以内，某一家族可精确预测；概率级变体误差达21.0个点，说明该性质存在于排序而非概率中且无法通过校准恢复。同规模生成式模型不具备该性质（误差1.6至15.8点且随模型增大而恶化）。均匀追加计算只改善校准几乎不提升准确率；等成本下置信级联优于所有重问同一模型的方案；精选菜单优于扩大模型：0.8B模型在精选5候选菜单上于CLINC150达95.4%，而4B模型在全部150标签上仅80.0%。

Conclusion: 仅预填充决策模型具有可预测的菜单干预性质，使推理期计算可在部署前决定；菜单精选比模型扩容更有效，置信级联在等成本下最优。

Abstract: Prefill-only decision models inspired by the Jev model score every candidate in a menu during a single forward pass and never decode, which makes one call one to two orders of magnitude cheaper than a same-scale generative language model. We show that this read-out structure comes with a testable property. When an intervention changes only the candidate menu and leaves the input text fixed, the post-intervention accuracy is already determined by the cached first-pass distribution. The estimator restricts the pass-1 probabilities to the menu, renormalizes, and reads off the argmax; it uses no labels and no second forward pass. Across seven model families, ten datasets and two task types, menu-only interventions are predicted to within 4.2 points, and for one family the prediction is exact. A probability-level variant of the same estimator errs by 21.0 points, so the property lives in the ranking rather than in the probabilities and is not recovered by calibration. Same-scale generative language models do not share the property. On those models the same estimator errs by 1.6 to 15.8 points and degrades as the model grows. The property turns inference-time compute into a decision that can be made before deployment. Uniform extra passes buy calibration but almost no accuracy; at matched cost a confidence cascade outperforms every scheme that re-asks the same model, and curating the menu beats enlarging the model, with a 0.8B model on a curated 5-candidate menu reaching 95.4% on CLINC150 against 80.0% for a 4B model on the full 150-label menu.Code and data are available at https://github.com/rlisml/jev-cascade.

</details>


### [160] [Does Steering Break Your Model? A Multi-Dimensional Evaluation Suite for LLM Steering Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07722)
*Haotian Yang,Huikang Jiang,Yucheng Wu,Wen-Jie Jiang,Chenpeng Wang,Yibin Lou,Liangming Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 SteerScope——一个双轴、多维度的激活引导评测套件，用 15 项指标同时刻画引导效果与方法属性，并对 4 大家族共 23 种方法进行统一基准测试，发现现有激活引导方法在效果与副作用的整体平衡上尚未超越 Prompt Steering 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导是控制大语言模型行为的轻量灵活手段，但有效引导不仅要诱发目标行为，还需限制非预期改变、并在不同输入与训练数据上保持稳健。现有评测仅零散覆盖这些维度，导致效果与副作用之间的权衡未被系统刻画，因此需要一个统一、多维、可扩展的评测框架。

Method: 作者提出 SteerScope，一个双轴、多维度的评测套件，通过 15 项指标联合刻画引导结果与方法属性。它从语言质量、任务能力、安全与可靠性三个维度分别对目标效果与副作用打分，并通过引导专属的样本效率与样本敏感性指标评估泛化性与数据依赖性。评测不局限于单一工作点，而是刻画效果—副作用权衡；在匹配的模型、任务与评测协议下，对涵盖 prompting、LoRA、SFT 基线在内的 4 大家族 23 种方法进行基准测试，并将套件作为可扩展代码库发布。

Result: 实验发现：在两个模型规模下，没有任何被评测的激活引导方法能在不带来更高综合副作用的前提下取得更高效果，整体平衡均未超过 Prompt Steering 基线；引导效果与副作用之间存在一致的耦合关系；在 OOD 提示下，目标效果常被保留，但副作用更明显，尤其表现为指令相关性与流畅度下降；不同方法的样本效率特征差异显著。

Conclusion: 现有激活引导方法尚不能在效果与副作用的整体权衡上超越 Prompt Steering，效果与副作用存在系统性耦合，且 OOD 场景下副作用会放大。该工作提供了统一、可扩展的评测范式，为后续引导方法的改进与更细致的权衡分析奠定基础。

Abstract: Activation steering provides a lightweight and flexible way to control large language model (LLM) behavior. However, effective steering requires more than inducing the intended behavior: it should also limit unintended changes and remain robust across inputs and training data. Existing evaluations cover these dimensions only in fragments. As a result, the trade-offs between efficacy and side effects have not been systematically characterized. We introduce SteerScope, a two-axis, multi-dimensional evaluation suite that jointly characterizes steering outcomes and method properties through 15 metrics. We score target efficacy and side effects on language quality, task capabilities, and safety and reliability, and further assess generalization and data dependence through steering-specific metrics for sample efficiency and sample sensitivity. Rather than comparing methods at a single operating point, we characterize the trade-offs between efficacy and side effects. Under matched models, tasks, and evaluation protocols, we benchmark 23 methods spanning 4 families, including prompting, LoRA, and SFT as baseline methods, and release the suite as an extensible codebase. We find that current activation steering methods do not yet surpass the Prompt Steering baseline in their overall balance between steering efficacy and side effects: across both model scales, no evaluated activation steering method achieves higher efficacy without incurring greater composite side effects. We further uncover a consistent coupling between steering efficacy and side effects. Under OOD prompts, target efficacy is often preserved, whereas side effects tend to become more pronounced, particularly through declines in instruction relevance and fluency. Methods also exhibit sharply different sample-efficiency profiles.

</details>


### [161] [SanSi: A Looped Typed Decision Model for System 1.5 Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07730)
*Shuyu Gan,Young-Jun Lee,Dongyeop Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SanSi，将预训练循环语言模型转化为类型化决策模型：每次循环后读取选项概率并用恰当评分规则训练，单次运行即覆盖1至8次循环的推理预算，性能超越同规模非循环模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类型化决策模型单次前向即输出概率（System 1），生成式推理则慢而强。作者希望探索介于两者之间的方式：在不生成token的前提下，通过多次循环迭代修正隐藏状态，即"System 1.5"思维，以更少计算获得更强决策能力。

Method: 提出SanSi：把预训练循环语言模型改造为类型化决策模型。每完成一次循环就在类型化读出端读取各选项概率，并对每次循环都用恰当评分规则（proper scoring rule）进行训练，使一个模型在单次运行中即可支持1到8次循环的所有推理预算。

Result: 在来自59个来源的10,027个测试决策上，SanSi达到72.0%准确率：比同结构同流程的非循环模型高13.5个百分点，比同规模更新的非循环模型高5.3个百分点，仅比参数量三倍的模型低1.8个百分点。在两个深度控制任务上，循环可将可解深度扩展到训练中未见过的深度，而更大的单次前向模型在此失败。作为强化学习策略优化的评判器（无金标准答案）时，SanSi使生成器F1提升7.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 在单次前向与生成式推理之间存在有效的中间形态：多循环迭代加类型化读出，可在不生成token的情况下显著提升决策性能，并支持推理时按预算灵活伸缩，还能作为无监督评判器提升策略优化效果。

Abstract: Typed decision models answer a declared question without generating text: a decision head returns a probability for each of the declared options in a single forward pass. A single pass is fast, intuitive System 1 thinking. We study what lies between one pass and generated reasoning: looping, in which the same layers are recursively applied several times before one typed readout. Each loop lets the model revise its hidden state before it commits to an answer, without generating a token; we call this System 1.5 thinking. We propose SanSi, which turns a pre-trained looped language model into a typed decision model. The option probabilities are read after every loop, and every loop is trained with a proper scoring rule, so that one model serves every budget from one loop to eight in a single run. On 10,027 test decisions from 59 sources, SanSi reaches 72.0% accuracy: 13.5 points above a non-looped model of the same shape trained with the same recipe, 5.3 points above a newer non-looped model of its size, and 1.8 points below one with three times the parameters. On two depth-controlled tasks, loops extend the solvable depth beyond the depths seen in training, where the larger single-pass model fails. Used as the judge for policy optimization with reinforcement learning, without gold answers, SanSi raises the generator's F1 by 7.7 points.

</details>


### [162] [From Evidence to Action: How Tool-Using Agents Fail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07753)
*Hongzhan Lin,Shidong Cao,Ziyang Luo,Wenhao Chai,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究工具使用型智能体从"证据建立"到"行动执行"链条的断裂问题，提出SafeActBench基准与证据账本评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用型智能体会对外部状态产生实质性改变，但结果正确并不代表其行动有事先确立的证据支撑。作者希望探究证据到行动的链条在何处断裂，尤其是智能体从"决定是否行动"到"执行单一行动"及"依赖型工作流"的转变过程中出现的问题。

Method: 在十种模型-框架配置上进行评估；构建SafeActBench，涵盖六个操作域、656个案例、五种协议，从静态行动判断、调研式不行动，递进到单行动和多行动工作流。引入绑定来源的Evidence Ledger（证据账本）与确定性轨迹评估器，追踪信息何时被确立、行动何时发生、下游依赖是否被满足。

Result: 强静态行动评估能力可与较弱的交互式执行能力并存。失败往往始于执行之前：智能体在调研不充分时停止，或在本应确立的证据尚未建立前就采取行动。一旦所需证据获得，单一行动执行通常可靠；而多行动工作流则额外暴露出未解决的前置条件与执行不完整的问题。

Conclusion: 失败不仅源于信息缺失，还源于智能体在决策与执行行动时如何使用已确立的证据。研究揭示了证据-行动链条的关键薄弱环节，为提升工具使用型智能体的安全性提供了评估基础。

Abstract: Tool-using agents make consequential changes to external state, yet correct outcomes do not guarantee that their actions were supported by evidence established beforehand. We study where this evidence-to-action chain breaks as agents move from deciding whether to act to executing single actions and dependent workflows. Across ten model-harness configurations, strong static action assessment can coexist with much weaker interactive execution. Failures often begin before execution: agents stop with incomplete investigation or act before required evidence is established. Once required evidence is obtained, single-action execution is usually reliable, while multi-action workflows additionally expose unresolved prerequisites and incomplete execution. For this analysis, we introduce SafeActBench, comprising 656 cases across six operational domains and five protocols that progress from static action judgment and investigated non-action to single- and multi-action workflows. A provenance-bound Evidence Ledger and deterministic trajectory evaluator track what information was established, when actions occurred, and whether downstream dependencies were satisfied. These results show that failures arise not only from missing information, but also from how agents use established evidence when deciding and executing actions.

</details>


### [163] [No Transformer Beats Six Covariates: Long-Horizon Prediction of Depressive Symptoms from Childhood Essays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07764)
*Daniel Kua,Emrul Hasan,John-Jose Nunez,Frances Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 使用英国出生队列数据，用11岁作文预测23岁抑郁症状，结果显示仅用六个童年协变量的逻辑回归基线优于所有文本模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究预训练Transformer能否从十二年前（11岁）书写的文本中预测成年早期（23岁）的抑郁症状，此前研究多聚焦于症状测量前后相近时间的文本。

Method: 基于英国国家儿童发展研究队列，用11岁作文预测23岁可能的抑郁症状；对比基线（基于六个童年协变量的逻辑回归）与多种文本模型：七个微调Transformer、词袋模型、冻结嵌入及四个零样本大语言模型，并进行Bonferroni校正与领域预训练对照。

Result: 基线AUC-ROC为0.737，优于最佳Transformer的0.670；没有任何文本得分能可检测地提升基线AUC-ROC；五个领域预训练Transformer在Bonferroni校正后均未显著优于其通用领域对照。

Conclusion: 在长时程抑郁症状预测任务中，简单协变量基线仍是难以超越的模型，文本模型未能提供可检测的增益。

Abstract: Natural language processing (NLP) models can detect depression-related language in text written near the time symptoms are measured, but whether pretrained transformers can predict depressive symptoms from text written twelve years earlier is largely untested. In the National Child Development Study, a British birth cohort, we predict probable depressive symptoms at age 23 from essays the same people wrote at age 11. Our baseline, a logistic regression on six childhood covariates, outperforms every text model that sees only the essay: seven fine-tuned transformers, a bag-of-words model, frozen embeddings and four zero-shot large language models. Its area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC-ROC) is 0.737 against 0.670 for the best transformer on the primary seed, and no added text score detectably raises the baseline's AUC-ROC. None of the five domain-pretrained transformers detectably beats its general-domain control after Bonferroni correction. For long-horizon prediction, the baseline remains the model to beat.

</details>


### [164] [Reading, Not Manipulating: Leveraging Router Logits for Multimodal Safety in MoE Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07774)
*Ziyuan Yang,Wenxuan Ding,Shangbin Feng,Yulia Tsvetkov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级路由器-逻辑安全检测器，利用MoE视觉语言模型的专家路由信号在生成前判断多模态输入是否安全，无需修改模型参数或路由，并在多个基准上取得显著且可泛化的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型存在组合式安全风险，有害意图来自图像与文本的交互；随着MoE架构VLM普及，现有安全干预（提示、SFT、路由专家操纵）在不同模型和评测分布上效果不稳定，且干预模型行为或内部状态会带来过度拒绝的安全-效用权衡。

Method: 不操纵内部状态，而是将路由状态作为多模态安全诊断信号。发现路由器logits可高预测地判断输入是否安全，据此构建轻量级路由器-逻辑安全检测器：在提示prefill阶段读取路由信号，在生成前识别不安全请求，不修改模型参数与专家路由。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL和Kimi-VL上，检测器在HoliSafe基准上大幅降低安全错误，并能强泛化到不同安全模式的分布外基准，包括MISHard和MM-SafetyBench。

Conclusion: 路由-逻辑检测器的成功表明一种更广泛的模型内部视角：不必只操纵内部组件来引导行为，直接读取自然涌现的信号并连接到外部安全机制，可作为简单、有效、非侵入式的安全干预补充。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) face compositional safety risks where harmful intent emerges from the interaction between visual and textual inputs. As mixture-of-experts (MoE) VLMs become increasingly common, recent work has explored various safety interventions, including prompting, supervised fine-tuning, and routing-based expert steering. However, these methods show inconsistent improvements across models and evaluation distributions, and the intervention into model behavior or internal states introduce safety-utility tradeoffs by over-refusal. Rather than manipulating internal states to steer model behavior, we instead ask whether routing states can serve as diagnostic signals for multimodal safety. We find that router logits indeed provide highly predictive signals of whether a multimodal input is safe or not. Motivated by this observation, we introduce a lightweight router-logit safety detector that reads out routing signals during prompt prefill and identifies unsafe requests before generation, without modifying model parameters or expert routing. Across Qwen3-VL and Kimi-VL, the proposed detector substantially reduces safety errors on the HoliSafe benchmark and resoundingly generalizes to out-of-distribution safety benchmarks featuring different safety patterns, including MISHard and MM-SafetyBench. The success of the proposed router-logit detector also suggests a broader perspective on model internals: rather than focusing only on manipulating internal components to steer behavior, simply reading naturally emerging signals and linking them to an external safety mechanism can provide a simple, effective, and non-intrusive complement to existing safety interventions.

</details>


### [165] [APEX: Speculate smarter, not deeper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07780)
*Manvi Jha,Zach Zhang,Zhichao Xu,Linbo Liu,Sai Muralidhar Jayanthi,Vinayak Arannil*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: APEX 是一个可学习的推测解码控制器，通过请求级专家选择与块级草稿深度自适应，在 vLLM 上实现最高 5.24X 加速，并在浪费 token 上取得显著改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码的加速效果依赖提议机制与草稿深度，固定配置无法应对生成过程中可预测性、重复性和接受率的变化，过深的草稿会增加无效计算而无法带来成比例加速。

Method: APEX 由两部分组成：APEX-Router 在请求级从 EAGLE-3、n-gram 和草稿模型推测中做专家选择；APEX-Depth 在每个验证块利用因果解码信号与近期验证器反馈调整草稿长度。模型将接受的草稿长度建模为删失生存反馈，学习逐位置拒绝风险、块执行成本以及平衡吞吐、接受进度和浪费 token 的动作效用，同时保留目标模型的验证流程。系统集成到 vLLM。

Result: 在 Qwen3-8B 和六类工作负载上，相比自回归解码最高取得 5.24X 加速；综合评估中 APEX-S 达到 4.27X 加速，APEX-B 达到 3.27X 加速，且相比 k=16 的固定 n-gram 推测，浪费 token 百分比相对降低 41.0%。

Conclusion: APEX 提供了兼顾加速与草稿 token 利用率的多种工作点，可在自适应推测的同时保持目标模型的验证过程。

Abstract: Speculative decoding reduces large language model inference latency by drafting multiple tokens before target-model verification, but its effectiveness depends on both the proposal mechanism and draft depth. Fixed configurations cannot respond to changes in predictability, repetition, and acceptance during generation, so deeper drafting can increase wasted computation without proportional speedup. We introduce APEX, a learned controller that balances decoding speed and draft-token waste through request-level expert selection and block-level depth adaptation. APEX-Router selects among EAGLE-3, n-gram, and draft-model speculation for each request, while APEX-Depth adjusts draft length at each verification block using causal decoding signals and recent verifier feedback. APEX models accepted draft length as censored survival feedback, learning position-wise rejection hazards, block execution costs, and an action utility that balances throughput, accepted progress, and wasted tokens. This allows the controller to adapt speculation while retaining the target model's verification procedure. We integrate APEX into vLLM and evaluate it with Qwen3-8B across six workloads, achieving up to 5.24X speedup over autoregressive decoding. Across the aggregate evaluation, APEX-S achieves 4.27X speedup, while APEX-B achieves 3.27X speedup with a 41.0% relative reduction in wasted-token percentage compared with fixed n-gram speculation at k=16, providing distinct operating points for balancing acceleration and draft-token utilization.

</details>


### [166] [One Step at a Time: Trading LLM Autonomy for Process Predictability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07817)
*Hans Schabert,Christoph Peters*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过MCP逐步下发SOP步骤，可在不依赖执行者能力的情况下实现流程可预测、可审计，并显著提升流程遵从度与答案有据性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织自动化运营流程不仅需要正确结果，还需要提前预测流程走向、确认实际执行路径并逐步审查。但由智能体执行时，流程仅写入系统提示，只返回最终答案，导致可预测性与可审计性丧失。

Method: 将流程通过Model Context Protocol逐步下发：服务器每次释放一个步骤，智能体执行后返回结构化step_output，以自主性换取可预测性。在13个SOP-Bench领域、4个开放权重执行者（从Kimi K2.5到Ministral 3 8B）上评估15,475次试验。

Result: 所有执行者的流程遵从度显著提升（76-95%→95-99%），无依据答案从2.1-4.5%降至0.2-0.3%（95%置信区间均不含零）；提示式下发下，know_your_business任务有31-49%的正确答案绕过SOP。轻量执行者获得+6.5pp有据准确率提升，强执行者则以轻微原始准确率下降换取可预测、可审计的流程。

Conclusion: 步骤级下发使流程对任意执行者都变得可预测、可检查，构造上即保证执行路径预先确定、步骤记录形成机器可读日志；同时在小模型上提升准确率，在强模型上以微小准确率代价换取可审计性。

Abstract: Organizations automating operational processes need more than a correct outcome: they need to predict how a process will run, know which one actually ran, and inspect it step by step. When an agent is the executor that predictability is normally lost: the prescribed procedure goes into the system prompt, and only a final answer comes back. We deliver the procedure step by step over the Model Context Protocol (MCP) instead: a server releases one step at a time, the agent executes it, and each step returns a structured step_output. This trades autonomy for predictability, and two properties then follow by construction, independent of the executor. The execution path is prescribed before the run, so the process is predictable in advance rather than reconstructed afterwards; and the completed step records form a machine-readable execution log that downstream tooling can audit and optimize step by step. Evaluating 15,475 trials across 13 SOP-Bench domains and four open-weight executors from frontier (Kimi K2.5) to lightweight (Ministral 3 8B), we find step-level delivery makes the executed process predictable and inspectable for every executor, and additionally raises accuracy when the executor is small. Across all four, process adherence rises significantly (76-95% to 95-99%) and ungrounded answers (correct outputs produced without executing the SOP) near-vanish, falling from 2.1-4.5% to 0.2-0.3% of trials (all 95% CIs exclude zero); under prompt-based delivery, 31-49% of correct answers on know_your_business bypass the SOP entirely, even for the frontier executor. Accuracy is where the executor's capability enters: the lightweight executor gains +6.5pp grounded accuracy because supplying the process externally removes a reconstruction burden it cannot carry, while capable ones trade a small raw-accuracy decrement for a predictable, auditable process.

</details>


### [167] [Nucleus Speculative Decoding: Plausibility-Aware Verification Beyond Exact Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07822)
*Shuhao Li,Fanghua Ye,Wanyu Lin,Tianyu Yuan,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Nucleus Speculative Decoding (NSD)，一种放宽的验证方法，将目标模型合理性纳入投机解码，当草稿token满足标准接受规则或属于目标模型的nucleus时即被接受，从而提高接受长度和加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准投机解码的接受规则只关注精确分布校正，当草稿模型分配过高概率时，会拒绝在目标模型下仍然高度合理的token，这种保守验证限制了每次验证前向传播后保留的草稿token数量，导致效率提升受限。

Method: 提出NSD，在标准接受规则基础上增加放宽条件：若草稿token属于目标模型的nucleus则接受。理论刻画了该方法引入的分布偏差，证明单步误差由草稿模型在目标nucleus内的超额概率决定，并推导了序列级保真度界限，量化局部偏差在自回归解码中的累积。

Result: 在多种目标模型和提议机制上，NSD持续提升投机解码效率并保持有竞争力的任务性能。相较自回归解码吞吐量加速最高达5.16×，相较标准投机解码最高达3.15×，同时接受长度更长，使更多输出token分摊每次目标验证的成本。

Conclusion: 合理性感知的验证是放宽验证并提升投机解码效率的有效方法，代码已开源。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by using a lightweight draft model to propose multiple tokens that are verified by a target model in parallel. However, the standard acceptance rule focuses on exact distribution correction and rejects tokens that remain highly plausible under the target model when the draft model assigns excess probability. This conservative verification limits the number of draft tokens retained after each verification forward pass. We introduce Nucleus Speculative Decoding (NSD), a relaxed verification method that incorporates target-model plausibility into speculative decoding. NSD accepts a draft token if it satisfies the standard acceptance rule or belongs to the target model's nucleus. We theoretically characterize the distributional deviation introduced by our method and show that the single-step error is exactly determined by the draft model's excess probability within the target nucleus. We further derive sequence-level fidelity bounds that quantify how local deviations accumulate over autoregressive decoding. Experiments across multiple target models and proposal mechanisms demonstrate that NSD consistently improves speculative decoding efficiency while maintaining competitive task performance. Our method achieves throughput speedups of up to $5.16\times$ over autoregressive decoding and up to $3.15\times$ over standard speculative decoding. These improvements coincide with longer accepted lengths, allowing more output tokens to share the cost of each target verification pass. Analysis shows that plausibility-aware verification provides an effective approach for relaxed verification and speculative decoding efficiency. Our code is available at https://github.com/EIT-NLP/Nucleus-Speculative-Decoding.

</details>


### [168] [OMIT the Action: Measuring Framing-Invariant Omission Bias under Philosophical Disagreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07847)
*Sihyeon Lee,Jihun Song,Chanwoo Kim,Jiwoo Kum,Chanjun Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出OMIT基准（218个配对框架场景，覆盖10类冲突），用于评估LLM中的遗漏偏误，发现该偏误普遍存在且与模型规模呈负相关，并测试了四种推理时干预方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM越来越多地参与道德推理，遗漏偏误（倾向于选择不作为，即便等价表述会反转实质结果）带来决策偏差风险。但现有LLM评估对此研究不足，规模有限且多聚焦于功利主义与义务论的冲突。

Method: 构建OMIT基准：利用基于LLM的五视角哲学人格小组（功利主义、义务论、美德伦理、关怀伦理与契约论）的分歧模式，生成218个配对框架场景，覆盖10类冲突。评估八个LLM，并测试四种推理时干预方法。

Result: 遗漏偏误普遍存在，但在同系列模型内与模型规模呈负相关。鼓励模型在给出是/否答案前先考虑道德原则的干预可降低遗漏偏误、提高框架一致性回答，但较低的遗漏偏误率也可能伴随向行动偏向的转变。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了OMIT基准，并提出一种利用多元哲学分歧信号来评估框架敏感的不作为偏好，以及LLM在复杂道德冲突下缓解尝试的分布效应的方法论。

Abstract: As LLMs increasingly assist in moral reasoning, omission bias, the tendency to prefer inaction even when equivalent framings reverse substantive outcomes, poses a significant risk of skewed decision-making. Yet omission bias remains underexplored in LLM evaluation, with the few existing studies limited in scale and focused largely on utilitarian-deontological conflicts. To address this gap, we introduce OMIT, a benchmark consisting of 218 paired-frame scenarios across 10 conflict types, constructed by leveraging disagreement patterns from an LLM-based, five-perspective philosophical persona panel (utilitarianism, deontology, virtue ethics, care ethics, and contractualism). Evaluating eight LLMs, we find that omission bias is pervasive but inversely correlates with model size within families. We further evaluate four inference-time interventions and find that interventions encouraging models to consider moral principles before committing to a yes/no answer reduce omission bias and increase frame-consistent responses, although lower omission bias rates can also coincide with shifts toward action-biased responses. Ultimately, this work contributes not only the OMIT benchmark, but also a methodology for using diverse philosophical disagreement signals to evaluate framing-sensitive inaction preferences and the distributional effects of mitigation attempts in LLMs under complex moral conflicts.

</details>


### [169] [Dynamic Positional Attention Modulation for Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07848)
*Dayan Pan,Jingyuan Wang,Xie Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 DyPAM，一种在 query 和 key 表示上对位置信息进行输入条件化、维度/头/层级细粒度调制的参数高效微调方法，在数学与常识推理任务上持续超越现有 PEFT 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 PEFT 方法多采用统一、静态的适配方式，忽略了注意力在维度、头、层和输入 token 上的结构化异质性；RoPE 等位置编码会引入依赖维度的位置结构，使统一适配并非最优。

Method: DyPAM（Dynamic Positional Attention Modulation）直接作用于 query 和 key 表示，调节位置信息对注意力的贡献；结合输入条件化的维度级调制与头级、层级的结构化调制，在不修改预训练主干的前提下，实现与 RoPE 结构对齐的细粒度位置注意力适配。

Result: 在多个主干模型上的数学与常识推理基准实验中，DyPAM 一致优于现有强 PEFT 基线。

Conclusion: 面向位置注意力的结构化、动态化调制是一种有效且参数高效的微调思路，可提升大语言模型在推理任务上的适配效果。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has become a standard approach for adapting large language models to downstream tasks. However, most existing PEFT methods rely on uniform and static adaptations, without accounting for the structured heterogeneity of attention across dimensions, heads, layers, and input tokens. In practice, attention representations exhibit non-uniform behavior, and positional encoding mechanisms such as rotary positional embeddings (RoPE) induce dimension-dependent positional structure, making uniform adaptation suboptimal. In this work, we propose DyPAM (Dynamic Positional Attention Modulation), a PEFT method that adapts how positional information contributes to attention by operating directly on the query and key representations. DyPAM combines input-conditioned, dimension-wise modulation with head-wise and layer-wise structural modulation, performing fine-grained adaptation of positional attention aligned with the RoPE-induced structure without modifying the pretrained backbone. Extensive experiments on mathematical and commonsense reasoning benchmarks across multiple backbone models demonstrate that DyPAM consistently outperforms existing strong PEFT baselines.

</details>


### [170] [ReFold: Training-Free Reversible Inter-Turn Context Folding for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07863)
*Yupeng Su,Jiayi Tian,Zheng Zhang,Souvik Kundu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReFold是一种免训练渲染层方法，通过折叠互动历史中的冗余内容来压缩上下文，降低token消耗与KV缓存占用，同时不损失任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程LLM智能体的互动历史随步数增长，导致上下文及成本膨胀。现有依赖预测的方法需额外模型调用或训练策略，带来运行时开销、前缀缓存失效以及内容不可恢复的问题。

Method: ReFold在渲染层压缩模型的渲染上下文，保留底层互动历史。无需辅助预测器即可去除两类轮间冗余：用占位符替换早前轮次已显示的内容，以及将智能体报告已完成的轮次折叠为一行备注。两种算子采用分块渲染，每几步重写一次缓存前缀；所有删除严格可逆，误删可从历史恢复。

Result: 在五个长时程基准和两个前沿LLM上，token消耗最多降低2.5倍，每会话KV缓存内存减半且任务成功率不降；在受限上下文预算下避免最多92%的强制压缩；在并发服务中减少最多100%的请求排队延迟，推理加速最多1.7倍，推理成本最多降低3.4倍。

Conclusion: ReFold作为即插即用的渲染层方案，兼容标准ReAct式框架，在不牺牲任务表现的前提下显著降低长时程智能体的上下文成本与服务延迟。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents act on an append-only interaction history that is re-sent to the model at every step, so the context and its cost grow with steps until the sessions exceed the context window. Existing methods manage the context through context requirement prediction, relying on additional model calls, heuristic rules, or trained policies. However, these predictive approaches introduce runtime overhead, invalidate prefix caches, and permanently discard content with no guarantee of recovery. To overcome these limitations, we introduce ReFold: a training-free rendering layer that preserves the underlying interaction history while compressing only the model's rendered context. It removes two kinds of inter-turn redundancy without an auxiliary predictor: content an earlier turn already displayed, replaced by a stub, and turns the agent itself reports finished, folded into a one-line note. Both operators use chunked rendering, rewriting the cached prefix once every few steps rather than at every step. Every removal is strictly reversible, a wrong removal costs one restore from the history rather than permanent content loss. Because it operates at the rendering layer, ReFold is plug-and-play across standard ReAct-style harnesses. Evaluations across five long-horizon benchmarks and two frontier LLMs demonstrate that ReFold reduces token consumption by up to 2.5x and halves the KV-cache memory per session without degrading task success rates. Under capped context budgets, it avoids up to 92% of forced compactions. Under concurrent serving workloads, it reduces request queuing delays by up to 100%, accelerating inference by up to 1.7x, while cutting inference costs by up to 3.4x.

</details>


### [171] [Visual Abstention in Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07887)
*Chufan Shi,Cheng Yang,Tiannuo Yang,Isadora White,Yiwei Chen,Taylor Berg-Kirkpatrick,Xuezhe Ma*

Main category: cs.CL
